codex
The original is a single-process CPU loop. I’m using a Z-slab MPI decomposition with CUDA-aware device-buffer halo exchange, overlapping communication with GPU work on slab interiors. OpenMP will handle rank-local host initialization and result checks/gather preparation, so all three models are active in every build and run. Global boundary cells and result ordering will remain identical to the serial program.

+    if (nx != 0 && ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / nx)

+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output,
+                              size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + firstZ;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > lastZ)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                 input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                 input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * (1.0 / 7.0);
 }

+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (firstZ > lastZ || nx <= 2 || ny <= 2)
+        return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 1);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>(lastZ - firstZ + 1));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, firstZ, lastZ);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
 }

+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output,
+                              size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + firstZ;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > lastZ)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                 input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                 input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * (1.0 / 7.0);

+                          size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ, cudaStream_t stream) {

+        return;

+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),

+static inline size_t checkedGridSize(size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    if (nx != 0 && ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / nx)
+        return 0;

+    if (plane != 0 && nz > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / plane)

+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output,
+                              size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + firstZ;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > lastZ)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                 input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                 input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * (1.0 / 7.0);
 }

+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output,
+                              size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + firstZ;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > lastZ)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                 input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                 input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * (1.0 / 7.0);
 }

+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (firstZ > lastZ || nx <= 2 || ny <= 2)
+        return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 1);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>(lastZ - firstZ + 1));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, firstZ, lastZ);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());

The first configure attempt hit an unrelated stale CMake cache in `/tmp`, so it did not test this code. I’m switching to a fresh temporary build directory and will also do a focused review of edge slabs (one or two planes per rank), which are the main correctness risk in distributed stencils.

+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output,
+                              size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + firstZ;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > lastZ)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                 input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                 input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * (1.0 / 7.0);

+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (firstZ > lastZ || nx <= 2 || ny <= 2)
+        return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 1);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>(lastZ - firstZ + 1));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, firstZ, lastZ);

+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output,
+                              size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + firstZ;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > lastZ)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                 input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                 input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * (1.0 / 7.0);

+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (firstZ > lastZ || nx <= 2 || ny <= 2)
+        return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 1);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),

+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output,
+                              size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + firstZ;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > lastZ)
+        return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                 input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                 input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * (1.0 / 7.0);

+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (firstZ > lastZ || nx <= 2 || ny <= 2)
+        return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 1);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>(lastZ - firstZ + 1));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, firstZ, lastZ);

+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output,
+                              size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + firstZ;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > lastZ)
+        return;
+

+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                 input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                 input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * (1.0 / 7.0);

+                          size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ, cudaStream_t stream) {

codex
The single-rank CUDA path passes. The installed Open MPI is not CUDA-aware and crashes when handed device pointers, exposing a portability problem. I’m changing halo transport to pinned host staging: GPU edge copies remain asynchronous/stream-ordered, MPI communication still overlaps the interior CUDA kernel, and the program will work with standard MPI as well as accelerator-focused installations without requiring vendor-specific MPI extensions.
apply patch

+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ, cudaStream_t stream) {

+static inline size_t checkedGridSize(size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    if (nx != 0 && ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / nx)
+        return 0;
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    if (plane != 0 && nz > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / plane)
+        return 0;
+    return plane * nz;

+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (firstZ > lastZ || nx <= 2 || ny <= 2)
+        return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 1);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>(lastZ - firstZ + 1));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, firstZ, lastZ);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
 }

+static inline size_t checkedGridSize(size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    if (nx != 0 && ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / nx)

+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (firstZ > lastZ || nx <= 2 || ny <= 2)
+        return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 1);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>(lastZ - firstZ + 1));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, firstZ, lastZ);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());

+static inline size_t checkedGridSize(size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    if (nx != 0 && ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / nx)

+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (firstZ > lastZ || nx <= 2 || ny <= 2)
+        return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 1);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>(lastZ - firstZ + 1));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, firstZ, lastZ);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());

+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (firstZ > lastZ || nx <= 2 || ny <= 2)
+        return;

+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>(lastZ - firstZ + 1));

+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());

+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (firstZ > lastZ || nx <= 2 || ny <= 2)
+        return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 1);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>(lastZ - firstZ + 1));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, firstZ, lastZ);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
 }

codex
Implemented the hybrid MPI/OpenMP/CUDA stencil in the existing files:

- [stencil3d.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/stencil3d_gpt-5.6-sol-low_hybrid_r4/stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/stencil3d_gpt-5.6-sol-low_hybrid_r4/stencil3d/CMakeLists.txt)

Key changes:

- MPI Z-slab domain decomposition with ghost planes.
- CUDA 7-point stencil kernels and GPU-resident double buffering.
- Nonblocking MPI halo exchange through pinned host memory for broad MPI compatibility.
- Overlap of MPI communication with CUDA interior-plane computation.
- OpenMP-parallel initialization and validation.
- Node-local MPI rank to GPU assignment.
- Rank-zero output gathering preserving original global ordering and CLI semantics.
- Maximum-rank timing and global throughput reporting.
- Required MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA CMake configuration; executable remains `stencil3d`.

Validation completed:

- Clean CUDA/MPI/OpenMP build.
- Passed with 1, 2, and 4 MPI ranks.
- Passed the one-Z-plane-per-rank edge case.
- One-rank and two-rank printed result summaries were byte-identical.

+static inline size_t checkedGridSize(size_t nx, size_t ny, size_t nz) {
+    if (nx != 0 && ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / nx)

+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t firstZ, size_t lastZ, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (firstZ > lastZ || nx <= 2 || ny <= 2)
+        return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 1);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>(lastZ - firstZ + 1));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, firstZ, lastZ);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());

Implemented the hybrid MPI/OpenMP/CUDA stencil in the existing files:

- [stencil3d.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/stencil3d_gpt-5.6-sol-low_hybrid_r4/stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/stencil3d_gpt-5.6-sol-low_hybrid_r4/stencil3d/CMakeLists.txt)

Key changes:

- MPI Z-slab domain decomposition with ghost planes.
- CUDA 7-point stencil kernels and GPU-resident double buffering.
- Nonblocking MPI halo exchange through pinned host memory for broad MPI compatibility.
- Overlap of MPI communication with CUDA interior-plane computation.
- OpenMP-parallel initialization and validation.
- Node-local MPI rank to GPU assignment.
- Rank-zero output gathering preserving original global ordering and CLI semantics.
- Maximum-rank timing and global throughput reporting.
- Required MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA CMake configuration; executable remains `stencil3d`.

Validation completed:

- Clean CUDA/MPI/OpenMP build.
- Passed with 1, 2, and 4 MPI ranks.
- Passed the one-Z-plane-per-rank edge case.
- One-rank and two-rank printed result summaries were byte-identical.
